Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the basics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 160 and the convergence rate is O(619/n^1).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_160(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 160. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^619(S, F) is trivial. QED.
